Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ceramics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 642. The resulting operator achieves bound O(63/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the ceramics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 642 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 63. QED.
